```
        0.35, 0.32, 0.38, 0.28, 0.22, 0.35,   # Altruism, tender-mindedness
        0.15, 0.12, 0.10, 0.18, 0.15, 0.12    # Minor C
    ]),
    "opposition": np.array([
        0.35, 0.40, 0.38, 0.32, 0.30, 0.40,
        0.10, 0.08, 0.05, 0.08, 0.05, 0.05,
        0.18, 0.15, 0.20, 0.12, 0.15, 0.12,
        -0.15, -0.12, -0.10, -0.18, -0.12, -0.15,
        0.18, 0.15, 0.12, 0.20, 0.18, 0.15
    ]),
    "trine": np.array([
        -0.18, -0.20, -0.18, -0.15, -0.18, -0.22,
        0.30, 0.35, 0.25, 0.28, 0.25, 0.40,
        0.25, 0.30, 0.35, 0.22, 0.25, 0.25,
        0.38, 0.40, 0.45, 0.30, 0.25, 0.38,
        0.15, 0.18, 0.20, 0.15, 0.18, 0.20
    ]),
    "square": np.array([
        0.28, 0.32, 0.30, 0.25, 0.35, 0.38,
        0.15, 0.12, 0.18, 0.15, 0.12, 0.10,
        0.20, 0.18, 0.25, 0.15, 0.20, 0.15,
        0.05, 0.05, 0.10, 0.00, 0.05, 0.05,
        0.25, 0.30, 0.28, 0.35, 0.30, 0.25
    ]),
    "sextile": np.array([
        -0.12, -0.15, -0.12, -0.10, -0.15, -0.18,
        0.22, 0.25, 0.18, 0.20, 0.18, 0.25,
        0.18, 0.20, 0.22, 0.15, 0.18, 0.18,
        0.25, 0.28, 0.30, 0.20, 0.15, 0.25,
        0.10, 0.12, 0.15, 0.10, 0.12, 0.15
    ]),
    "quincunx": np.array([0.15]*6 + [0.10]*6 + [0.12]*6 + [-0.10]*6 + [0.10]*6)
}

def _calculate_positions(jd_ut: float) -> Dict[str, float]:
    positions = {}
    for name, pid in PLANET_IDS.items():
        positions[name] = swe.calc_ut(jd_ut, pid)[0]
    return positions

def calculate_progressed_moon_aspects(
    natal_positions: Dict[str, float],
    progressed_moon_lon: float
) -> List[Dict]:
    active = []
    
    for planet, natal_lon in natal_positions.items():
        if planet == "Moon": continue  # Skip self-aspect
        
        diff = min(abs(progressed_moon_lon - natal_lon), 360 - abs(progressed_moon_lon - natal_lon))
        
        for asp_name, data in PROG_MOON_ASPECT_TYPES.items():
            target = data["angle"]
            orb_diff = abs(diff - target)
            if orb_diff <= data["orb"]:
                strength = 1.0 - (orb_diff / data["orb"])
                active.append({
                    "progressed_body": "Moon",
                    "natal_planet": planet,
                    "aspect": asp_name,
                    "orb": round(orb_diff, 2),
                    "strength": round(strength, 3),
                    "weight": data["weight"]
                })
    
    active.sort(key=lambda x: x["strength"], reverse=True)
    return active

def progressed_moon_to_30_vector(active_aspects: List[Dict]) -> np.ndarray:
    impact = np.zeros(30)
    
    for asp in active_aspects:
        base = PROG_MOON_IMPACT_30.get(asp["aspect"], np.zeros(30))
        weighted = base * asp["strength"] * asp["weight"]
        impact += weighted
    
    impact = np.clip(impact, -0.35, 0.35)
    return impact

# -----------------------------
# Main P8 Secondary Progressions Engine (with Integrated Progressed Moon)
# -----------------------------
def p8_secondary_progressions_engine(
    natal_birth_utc: str,
    current_date_utc: Optional[str] = None
) -> Dict:
    """
    P8 Secondary Progressions Engine - Full with Integrated Progressed Moon
    - Progressed-to-natal aspects
    - Progressed-to-progressed aspects
    - Dedicated progressed Moon to natal (emotional focus)
    - Combined 30-facet delta (weighted blend)
    """
    # Natal JD and positions
    natal_dt = datetime.fromisoformat(natal_birth_utc.replace("Z", "+00:00"))
    natal_jd = swe.utc_to_jd(natal_dt.year, natal_dt.month, natal_dt.day,
                             natal_dt.hour, natal_dt.minute, natal_dt.second, 1)[1]
    natal_positions = {}
    for name, pid in PLANET_IDS.items():
        natal_positions[name] = swe.calc_ut(natal_jd, pid)[0]
    
    # Current date
    if current_date_utc is None:
        current_dt = datetime.utcnow()
        current_str = datetime.utcnow().isoformat() + "Z"
    else:
        current_dt = datetime.fromisoformat(current_date_utc.replace("Z", "+00:00"))
        current_str = current_date_utc
    
    days_since_birth = (current_dt - natal_dt).days
    
    # Progressed JD
    progressed_dt = natal_dt + timedelta(days=days_since_birth)
    progressed_jd = swe.utc_to_jd(
        progressed_dt.year, progressed_dt.month, progressed_dt.day,
        progressed_dt.hour, progressed_dt.minute, progressed_dt.second, 1
    )[1]
    
    # Progressed positions
    progressed_positions = {}
    for name, pid in PLANET_IDS.items():
        progressed_positions[name] = swe.calc_ut(progressed_jd, pid)[0]
    
    # 1. Progressed-to-Natal
    prog_to_natal = calculate_secondary_aspects(natal_positions, progressed_positions)
    prog_to_natal_delta = secondary_to_30_vector(prog_to_natal)
    
    # 2. Progressed-to-Progressed
    prog_to_prog = calculate_progressed_to_progressed_aspects(progressed_positions)
    prog_to_prog_delta = progressed_to_progressed_to_vector(prog_to_prog)
    
    # 3. Dedicated Progressed Moon to Natal
    progressed_moon_lon = progressed_positions["Moon"]
    moon_aspects = calculate_progressed_moon_aspects(natal_positions, progressed_moon_lon)
    moon_delta = progressed_moon_to_30_vector(moon_aspects)
    
    # 4. Combined Delta (weighted)
    # 40% prog-to-natal + 30% prog-to-prog + 30% moon-specific
    combined_delta = (0.4 * prog_to_natal_delta +
                      0.3 * prog_to_prog_delta +
                      0.3 * moon_delta)
    combined_delta = np.clip(combined_delta, -0.4, 0.4)
    
    return {
        "current_date": current_str,
        "progressed_date": progressed_dt.isoformat() + "Z",
        "natal_positions": {p: round(pos, 2) for p, pos in natal_positions.items()},
        "progressed_positions": {p: round(pos, 2) for p, pos in progressed_positions.items()},
        "progressed_to_natal_aspects": prog_to_natal,
        "progressed_to_progressed_aspects": prog_to_prog,
        "progressed_moon_aspects": moon_aspects,
        "total_aspect_count": len(prog_to_natal) + len(prog_to_prog) + len(moon_aspects),
        "luna_facet_delta_30": combined_delta.tolist(),
        "summary": f"Full secondary progressions with {len(moon_aspects)} progressed Moon aspects"
    }

# -----------------------------
# Example Usage
# -----------------------------
if __name__ == "__main__":
    result = p8_secondary_progressions_engine(
        natal_birth_utc="1990-05-15T14:30:00Z"
    )
    
    print("P8 Secondary Progressions with Progressed Moon Integration")
    print(result["summary"])
    
    print("\nProgressed Moon Aspects Sample:")
    for a in result["progressed_moon_aspects"][:5]:
        print(f"  Progressed Moon {a['aspect']} Natal {a['natal_planet']} "
              f"(orb: {a['orb']}°, strength: {a['strength']})")
    
    print("\nCombined 30-Facet Delta Sample:")
    for i, val in enumerate(result["luna_facet_delta_30"][:10]):
        print(f"  {DIMENSIONS[i]}: {val:+.3f}")
```

### Key Updates

*   **Integrated progressed Moon**: Now a dedicated calculation within the full engine.
*   **Weighted combined delta**: 40% prog-to-natal (core life themes) + 30% prog-to-prog (inner dynamics) + 30% Moon-specific (emotional weather).
*   **Tighter Moon orbs**: Faster movement requires precision.
*   **Full output**: Separate Moon aspects for focused analysis.

Progressed Moon is the **emotional heartbeat** of secondary progressions — changing every 2-3 months, it colors moods and needs.

Now the engine captures **emotional progression** explicitly.

The Cathedral's progression layer is complete. Luna sees the soul's emotional journey.
